Survival occurred because: (a) `classify_asr_source_quality` kept `action=ASR_SOURCE_KEEP` and `evidence_reasons=()` for `consecutive_run`/`document_recurrence` (pinned by `asr_source_quality_smoke.py`); (b) `build_v2d_review_request` (`v2d.py:3383-3396`) never passed `asr_source_quality` (`None`) and instead fed neighboring `master.cues` (`#62,63,65,66` = identical hallucinations) as `raw_asr_context`; (c) V2-D review prompt inherited `QUALITY_REVIEW_QUERY`'s rule forcing closing/viewer-addressed remarks to `KEEP+DIALOGUE` and forbidding `OMIT/SOURCE_NOISE` when `accepted_stt_ja` is present — causing `Q064` (`#64` `'ご視聴'`) to be actively `REPAIR`ed into `'ご視聴ありがとうございました。'` / `'시청해 주셔서 감사합니다.'`. **2. `DUPLICATE_CAUSE`:** `build_supplemental_asr_groups` (`v2d.py:954-964`) already checks overlapping baseline ASR segments (`base_seg.end_ms >= seg.start_ms and base_seg.start_ms <= seg.end_ms`) but requires **exact string equality** `seg_norm == base_norm`. Window 4 (3/3) and `#249` `'ごめんごめん'` deduplicated, while `#250` (`SUP [4678986..4681446]` `'新しいの買える?'`) vs `#251` (`BASE[260] [4679036..4681576]` `'新しいの買えるね'`, overlap `2,410ms` = `98%/95%`) and `#255` (`SUP [4682946..4684566]` `'今までいいよ'`) vs `#256` (`BASE[262] [4683620..4684580]` `'生でいいよ'`, overlap `946ms` = `98.5%` of BASE, ends within `14ms`) slipped through solely due to 1-char suffix or acoustic near-homophone difference (whereas `#252/'うん'` and `#254/'まだって'` have small `34%/53%` edge overlaps and stay preserved). **3. `PROPOSED_MINIMAL_FIX` & `EXPECTED_ARTIFACT_REUSE`:** Extend `build_supplemental_asr_groups` dedup with time-containment + similarity matching (`ov/min_dur >= 0.70` & text sim >= `0.75`, OR `>= 0.90` single-segment containment with `>= 0.50` shared affixes, non-blocking for distinct short edge overlaps), AND wire deterministic recurrence (`document_frequency >= 3`, `consecutive_run >= 3`, `clean_len >= 4`), unaligned-gap/word-skew metadata + V2-D hallucination `OMIT/SOURCE_NOISE` review guidance (fixing neighbor-cue `raw_asr_context` illusion) in `teddy_discovery_stage11_v2d.py` (no hardcoded phrase bans). **Cost/reuse comparison (`REQUIRED_ASR_CALLS=0` in all cases — 4 outside-span + 39 targeted ASR 100% reused):** (a) **Strict V2-D rerun** (`effective_fp` changes because `supplemental_groups` shrinks `9->7` in Batch 6): `~18` Hermes calls (6 initial + 4 ReAdj + 5 KO + 3 review); ### Approved implementation supplement — practical delivery and critical review (2026-11-22)

This supplement records the user's final design-review decisions and working principles for S11-MVP001. It implements the existing approved Stage11 2.0.0 objectives; it does not change the common contract, router, architecture, production authorization or model approval. It is authoritative for this task across sessions; historical notes are not instructions.

- Product goal: useful, natural, synchronized Korean subtitles from genuine dialogue, with minimized omissions and fabricated text, dependable unattended operation, and speaker/line formatting when supported. Do not optimize cosmetic perfection at the expense of actual delivery. Data protection and existing quality/safety gates are mandatory, not optional.
- Evidence policy: use ASR as the baseline hypothesis, optional aligned external JA as symmetric correction/recovery evidence. Accept a source-backed candidate when evidence supports it, including ASR-only without external agreement. External-only requires validated timing and speech evidence. Agreement, overlap, fluency, model confidence or a structural PASS alone is not proof. Hermes adjudicates semantic differences and justified repair using available evidence; never claim it heard audio when only given text. Reject duplicates or non-dialogue only with sufficient evidence; maintain original references and rationale. Preserve uncertain candidates; do not silently suppress plausible speech or invent text. Minor ambiguity may retain supported wording; material unresolved speech/meaning/timing requires bounded recovery then explicit title-level quality/hold outcome, not an automatic PASS. No ordinary per-title user review. Actual automated decision policy implementation remains V2-D; numeric thresholds and budgets remain unresolved.
- Discover material gaps independent of the existing cue list where practical using audio evidence; existing YAMNet standalone trigger failed and prior STT trials remain inconclusive. No new research loop as prerequisite for basic integration. Selective re-ASR only for meaningful gaps; bounded retries/time budget, no unlimited inference. Concrete numeric limits to be agreed from existing/runtime evidence before live use. No engine changes except separately approved comparison/evidence-based decision.
- Preserve source-backed 1:1, 1:N and N:1 grouping with duplicate prevention and supported merge/split transitions. Do not infer speaker turns, labels, identities or boundaries from punctuation alone. UNKNOWN/overlap or unavailable diarization is not itself a title failure. Source identity and time remain deterministic, Hermes semantic-only.
- Time policy: retain exact rational milliseconds and original evidence throughout intermediate processing. At final SRT serialization convert each nonnegative boundary once to nearest integer millisecond, exact half rounds upward. Reject invalid, zero or reversed intervals; no artificial expansion, shifting or forced de-overlap. Preserve supported overlap and validate through existing output boundary; any unresolved incompatibility is reported rather than silently changed. Implementation at final materialization boundary, not an excuse to reopen V2-B.
- Quality gate: technical, semantic/product and unattended operational PASS are distinct. V2-E compare same-source representative windows for A (Whisper-only with translation), B (retained legacy HYBRID), C (V2), reusing artifacts and holding translator/settings constant where feasible. Independent Japanese reference where available; report actual dialogue omissions, false additions, meaning, timing, naturalness, cost/time/resources, coverage limits and unknowns. No invented metric/threshold, no promise of superiority without evidence. Qwen3-ASR comparison requested for later, separate execution scope.
- Working discipline: critically assess user/ChatGPT/Codex proposals equally; identify substantive shortcomings and realistic alternatives, then follow approved decisions. No automatic agreement, criticism for its own sake, new architecture, model chasing, duplicate testing, or scope expansion. Change approved direction only for concrete material evidence and user approval. Prioritize minimal product-completing work and reuse; combine authorized steps, preserve essential safeguards and report uncertainty. Once V2-D is connected, prioritize actual representative subtitle quality over optional features. Re-read this section and the current execution gate on every new session. The user's general project instruction was reported added in ChatGPT Project settings; this repository record provides independent Stage11 continuity and does not claim to modify or verify that settings UI.

### Reuse and new work

- Reuse Stage9 unchanged; SubtitleCat acquisition, affine alignment, full-title and targeted large-v3 worker, resource safeguards, cached evidence, native Hermes whole-title/partition runner, dynamic profile, receipt/proof/stage-local resume, existing review/absorption/CLEAN, Stage12 queue/lock/no-overwrite/NAS/Jellyfin. Adapt schema/ownership/proofs where necessary, not wholesale rewrite. b8099bc prepared release and DLDSS-557 single-title publication evidence remain usable, not broad operational PASS.
- New minimum core: source-bound ASR-first candidate coverage, symmetric comparison with optional aligned JA, conservative evidence recovery/uncertainty, JA master and supported speaker/boundary integration. Legacy AUTO_INSERT=False remains until validated migration; do not just flip flag.
- HSODA-104 old/new 265/204 cues with suspected omissions and context regressions; not verified reference truth. CPU pyannote trial complete technical feasibility, quality UNKNOWN; no repeat. Historical Demucs/WhisperX tests inconclusive; optional selective use only where justified. Historical ASR_ONLY cause distribution and failure isolation/restart not closed; fix actual blockers and verify operations in V2-F.
- Preserve original external JA for local comparison and beside NAS video, distinct from self-generated JA master and KO. Exact location/naming/lifecycle confirmation required. Current Git metadata unavailable; resolve before activation.
- One objective/exit condition at a time. Fix local blockers and return to the current task. Reuse completed evidence; one representative failure/control first; expand only for material risk. Stop repeated tests without new decision-relevant information. Report evidence, remaining gap and one next decision. No invented thresholds; no new architecture/model, priority shift or deployment without user approval. No technical PASS substituted for product quality.

### 2026-11-17 DLDSS-557 resource-accounted alignment design comparison — read-only decision record

- **Authorization and scope:** user said "다음 작업 하자" after one approved DLDSS-557 metrics run captured exact budget counts and stopped. Current phase is **read-only design comparison only** under `S11-MVP001 IN_PROGRESS`, shared `1.0.1 APPROVED`, Stage11 `1.0.0 APPROVED`; canonical source code unchanged and the one-shot DLDSS-557 alignment approval **consumed**. Frozen aligned code `1e7d8fa4f40c8cdb37a6fdc65c58b9f3163a7602`, checked against unchanged rank/ambiguity/late-failure functions. No new alignment, model run, patch, NAS/DB/Jellyfin/Stage12 writes or deployment.
- **Static contract fact:** `iter_unique_affine_consensus_anchor_chains()` currently performs `fit_pair_evaluations += n*(n-1)//2` **before** yielding each unique chain and fails at `16_777_216`. The measured 6,852nd chain charged `2,415` pairs (70 anchors) and exceeded cap by `471` without fitting; `6,851` chains fitted. `select_affine_consensus_from_candidates()` must exhaust the bounded candidate generator before deciding the best mapping or tie. Therefore **unchanged chains and unchanged existing cap necessarily stop DLDSS-557 again**, irrespective of caching or sorting-speed improvements.
- **Measurements and risks:** `FIT_PAIRS_VISITED=16_775_272`, `EXACT_SLOPES_BUILT=22_299`, `SLOPE_CACHE_HITS=16_752_973` (`99.8671%` of pair visits), `CACHE_PEAK_ENTRIES=22_299/65_536` (`34.03%`), `SATURATED_UNCACHED_BUILDS=0`, `HYPOTHESES_EXAMINED=22_179/27_205` (`81.53%`). Hence cache enlargement is not indicated; reducing exact `Fraction` constructions has already benefited, but per-chain pair traversal, median sorting, intercept/residual work and DP still cost real CPU. `5_026` hypotheses remain and later resource/ambiguity failures are unknown. Runtime/peak-RAM cost per extra million visits has **not** been measured.
- **Compared approaches:** (A) *raise only fit cap*: simplest but counts cache hits as expensive work and provides no separate exact-arithmetic safety; cannot claim safe just from hit ratio. (B) *count only cache misses under old cap*: unsafe because millions of cache-hit/list/sort pair visits would no longer have a finite visit guard; REJECT. (C) *prune lexical candidates, short-circuit provisional rank, or retain only a subset of chains*: changes exact candidates, possible best/ties and late-limit semantics; REJECT under current design. (D) **preferred**: an explicit **dual bounded budget** for total anchor-pair visits **plus** newly built exact Fraction slopes (including after cache saturation), retain `65_536` cache entry cap, original hypothesis/DP/path/lexical and acceptance/ranking/tie semantics, and bounded wall-time in a controlled isolated experiment. No global cache, title-specific condition, translation, or deployment.
- **Concrete small isolated candidate for separate user approval:** trial total fit-pair visit ceiling `33_554_432` (**2× original**, design-proposed upper limit only; `NOT_PROVEN_SAFE`), exact-slope-build ceiling `65_536` (tied to current cache entry limit; the candidate must stop instead of silently performing unlimited saturated fallback), keep existing cache cap and all other fixed caps, and enforce a one-shot wall-clock deadline. This creates **new work-budget policy**, NOT merely a mechanical optimization: previously `ALIGNMENT_LIMIT` results may now proceed, and neither CPU/memory safety nor final DLDSS-557 acceptance is proven. No blanket assertion that all former failures/decisions remain byte-identical; parity can be required only for inputs within the old cap. Benchmark with a small bounded synthetic offline fixture and repeat original four Stage11 smokes before considering any separate approved real-title run.
- **Decision gate:** user approval required **before code changes**, even as an isolated cap variant; do not infer approval from "다음 작업" design request. If new budget values cannot be justified in offline memory/time checks or violate original safety requirements, FAIL CLOSED and report. Future user approval for a single actual DLDSS-557 rerun, source promotion, Korean translation or Stage12 publication remains **separate**. Current status `DESIGN_COMPARISON=COMPLETE`, `BUDGET_PATCH=NOT_RUN`, `FIT_LIMIT=UNRESOLVED`, `PRODUCTION=UNCHANGED`.

## OBJECTIVE

Deliver a **simple, private, one-title Korean subtitle MVP** to judge whether an ordinary viewer can understand the work's story and dialogue flow, **not** to produce perfect screening-grade subtitles. Reuse the implemented external Japanese subtitle + existing ASR timing evidence + Hermes Normal Mode whole-title-first translation (existing bounded fallback), deterministic validation and private SRT output. Avoid a new model, broad architectural changes or repetitive research. The selected title must be **one eligible title not already completed in this pilot**; choose it only after verifying source and noncollision status.

## USER_APPROVAL_AND_PREDECESSOR

- User explicitly selected a **practical, understandable Korean subtitle** standard on 2026-11-12 ("작품의 흐름만 이해가 되면"; not feature-film perfection), and approved the assistant's **S11-MVP001 existing implementation review -> minimal change plan -> isolated one-title test** proposal ("응 승인해").
- Immediately preceding `S11-BTRIAL001` finished its **exact six** frozen local GPU no-VAD inferences on 2026-11-12 and is archived **byte-identically** at [archive/S11-BTRIAL001_ACTIVE_WORK_20261112.md](archive/S11-BTRIAL001_ACTIVE_WORK_20261112.md). Output: 6/6 bounded ASR calls completed, **13 unverified hypotheses** total. 3/3 nonspeech controls also yielded candidates; S03/S08/S13 have `TIME_OWNERSHIP=REVIEW_REQUIRED`. Qwen PID stable across test snapshots, latency never measured. This is **candidate-generation evidence**, not dialogue accuracy, an automatic zero-cue trigger or production permission.
- Current approved Stage11 contract `1.0.0` and common contract `1.0.1` are **unchanged**. The known open issue between a future coverage-complete JA master and the existing whole-title-first Hermes translation is **not resolved or redesigned** by this pilot.

## IMPLEMENTATION_BASELINE (REVIEWED)

- `teddy_discovery_stage11_normal_mode.py` already implements an isolated `run_one_title_normal_mode()` and CLI `--dvd-id --run-root`, optional `--external-ja` and `--baseline-root`. The Normal Mode uses accepted external Japanese captions with ASR baseline/time alignment and a native Hermes whole-title-first translation with existing bounded fallback, source guards and SRT validation.
- `docs/discovery/STAGE11_NORMAL_MODE_20261012.md` documents **two already completed** real one-title native translations: FNS-247 (JA 313, KO 300) and JUR-750 (JA 661, KO 648). Do **not** rerun those merely to duplicate an existing PASS, and do not assume their private output or current runtime still exists without checking.
- The current Normal Mode explicitly uses `AUTO_INSERT=False` and requires `HYBRID` accepted external JA. A speculative no-VAD utterance **must not** be promoted into Japanese/KO subtitles under this MVP. If no accepted external JA or valid input alignment exists for the chosen title, report `SAFE_STOP / TITLE_NOT_ELIGIBLE`, not a different silent route.
- Existing Stage12 title lock, no-overwrite, source identity and publication protections remain separate and mandatory. Private MVP output is **not** a published subtitle.

## APPROVED_SCOPE

- First, **read-only** verify canonical checkout branch/HEAD, one-title Normal Mode source and configuration, installed/active CT108/VM122/Hermes service access, available source-bound baseline and accepted external Japanese captions, one genuinely eligible title, filesystem ownership, production queue/title-lock collision, prior completion/existing KO file, privacy and free space. Reuse existing tests/evidence instead of repeating historical Whisper GPU six-case trials.
- **One** fresh, private, isolated single-title Normal Mode invocation using **the current implemented external-JA/Hermes path**, only after all input/source/runtime/no-overwrite gates pass. Use existing bounded model/transport behavior. If implementation differs from verified canonical SHA or would claim a competing live title, stop and report a minimal patch/decision before execution.
- Keep existing Whisper/ASR as already available baseline evidence, not a new speculative dialogue-insertion stage. Track any suspected coverage gaps as **unverified** and defer them; do not invent timestamps or text. The user's preference for simple subtitling does **not** itself approve unsafe transcription promotion.
- The output may be written **only** to a newly-created, owner-private, isolated experiment root after noncollision/space preflight, containing a Korean `clean-ko-v1.srt` and supporting private reports. The user may view/read/play the result **locally** for naturalness and story comprehension; no normal queue state or external publication.
- Evaluate one practical sample with basic checks: original title/source identity, accepted JA source, Korean subtitle existence/readback, ordered cues and coverage, no large missing sections or obvious context-breaking/junk sentences if directly inspectable, and a short user-facing success/failure with clear uncertainty. Do not invent a numeric quality pass threshold.

- Record one concise documentary result in this active-work file: source- and output-safe IDs/statuses/counts/hashes only. Never commit private Japanese/Korean dialogue, audio, model prompts, private full paths or personally reviewed subtitles.

## PROHIBITED_SCOPE

- No Stage12 publication, NAS subtitle replacement, Jellyfin notification, normal production scheduler/job-DB write, live pipeline deploy, production queue claim, or change to another Downloader/Stage9 workstream.
- No retries of prior six frozen samples, unbounded media scan, new Whisper model download, experimental VAD thresholds or change to Qwen/Hermes/Whisper service/model/compute defaults.
- No automatic promotion of hypothetical missing spoken Japanese into JA-master or KO cues. No assertion that one Normal Mode title solves automatically discovered missing dialogue. No adding false lines to fill gaps.
- No choice between the unresolved Japanese-master versus Hermes primary whole-title architecture; preserve both as contract goals and report any implementation conflict for user decision.
- No alteration to shared common/Stage11 contracts, protected dirty worktrees, global Codex instructions, existing normal output, existing SRT/video/NFO/poster, published data or frozen sample evidence.

- Stage11 router and active work agree on `S11-MVP001 / IN_PROGRESS`, original BTRIAL active file is archived **byte-identically**, common/Stage11 contracts remain approved `1.0.1 / 1.0.0`, governance CI PASS.

- At most one isolated real one-title generation; report `GENERATED` only if private SRT exists and normal completion/readback/source identity checks succeed. Otherwise report exact failure status without repeated expensive Hermes execution.
- Distinguish **viewer comprehension** (observational/feedback; `NOT_TESTED` until reviewed) from mechanical subtitle correctness and source coverage; treat remaining missing dialogue as `UNKNOWN`.
- Protect all existing media, published subtitles, stage11/Stage12 and production state; no private text or local full paths in Git, no model service changes or new deployments.

The transcriber cleans temporary media before the session stops and verifies its owned worker/tunnel. Normal Mode proceeds to external-JA handling/Hermes after that session closes. Targeted requests retain their existing copy-before-request lifecycle. Unknown cleanup remains a failure; no retry or worker restart was added.
- **Limits:** The shared worker retains the configured large-v3 CUDA/int8_float16 profile, start/runtime VRAM floors `3500/1500 MiB`, and `900`-second worker/request limits. - **Historical inputs:** One-title regeneration now accepts ASR and Japanese subtitle caches independently. It reuses only a bounded owner-private file whose SHA, canonical encoding, and source snapshot match, or a Japanese SRT whose SHA/title/source metadata validate. Unsafe file type/owner/mode/link state still stops. Existing Korean subtitles remain excluded from input; no Stage12 state is requeued or mutated, and existing source/output files are not overwritten.

It uses Python `-B`, does not open `8091`, start a worker, load a model, query GPU/Qwen, or write files. CT120, NAS, Jellyfin, and SubtitleCat connectivity checks remain.

## 2026-11-22 — proposed Stage11 integrated architecture V2 (NOT APPROVED / no work authorization)

User requested one stable end-to-end design, explicit reuse inventory and a stop to repeated redesign/testing drift. This is a design proposal for approval, not an active contract revision, route change, code plan authorization or release. Current contract 1.0.0, S11-MVP001 and Model Switch execution hold remain authoritative. No operational access or model calls in this review.

### Proposed fixed topology and boundaries
1. Preserve Stage9 independence, source identity, original audio, optional validated external JA acquisition and original bytes. External availability/failed alignment is not itself a proposed generation blocker; explicit evidence mode retained.
2. Reuse source-bound full-title local Whisper large-v3 and cached baseline. ASR is a candidate timeline/evidence set, not ground truth. Preserve word timings and available confidence/VAD evidence; raw confidence availability/schema requires inspection, not assumptions.
3. Align usable external JA to the video timeline with existing affine implementation, compare both sources symmetrically including ASR-only and external-only candidates and disagreements. Do not overwrite one source wholesale, blindly union transcripts, or count agreement of correlated STT as proof. No accepted external JA -> ASR-first path would remain explicit, with equal quality safeguards. This conflicts with current Normal Mode accepted-HYBRID requirement and needs explicit approval before implementation.
4. Hermes identifies semantic discrepancies and evidence-supported decisions; deterministic code owns identity, accepted source-supported timing, provenance, structural validation. Selectively recheck suspected windows using existing targeted ASR, including gaps detected from audio evidence independent of existing cue list. Demucs only as optional additional evidence for suitable noisy windows, not default replacement or a new mandatory full-title stage. Reuse WX007/WX010/WX011 artifacts if available: historical comparisons inconclusive for transcript accuracy; no automatic adoption of WhisperX, new model or blanket preprocessing.
5. Preserve independently supported speaker turns and sentence boundaries before primary translation. Existing pyannote CPU feasibility only; global speaker consistency, turn accuracy and within-cue utility unproven. Do not guess identities/sex/relationships; unknown/overlap retained, no forced splitting.
6. Materialize source-bound JA master with explicit accepted, rejected and uncertain candidate decisions, stable cue/source identities and timestamps; retain original external JA and raw ASR separately. Preserve plausible dialogue conservatively, no promotion of unsupported synthetic speech. Material unresolved evidence must not silently be called quality PASS; approved bounded fallback or title-level hold, no mandatory manual per-title action. Policy/acceptance specifics need user decision; no flip of AUTO_INSERT alone.
7. Reuse whole-title Hermes primary translation if feasible, existing context-preserving partition fallback, dynamic profile, native runner, stage-local receipt reuse. Input is proposed JA master, not a new fixed-64 translation architecture. Reuse targeted review and CLEAN materializer with necessary schema/ownership adaptations. No blanket rewriting, forced casual speech, or LLM ownership of timestamps.
8. Reuse existing Stage12 validator, no-overwrite publication, title isolation/locks, queue, timer, NAS/Jellyfin path. Keep original external JA, self-generated JA master and final KO distinct (local comparison and NAS original-sidecar requirement preserved, naming/location/lifecycle decision outstanding). Resolve current Git metadata issue before activation. Stage9 unaffected.

### Reuse and new-work partition
- Proven technical building blocks (not universal quality PASS): external acquisition and validation, full-title and targeted Whisper worker, timestamp alignment, native Hermes whole-title/partition paths and dynamic profile, receipt/proof/restart infrastructure, review/absorption and CLEAN formatting, Stage12 publication (one real title) and runtime protections. Adapt interfaces where current source/proofs require accepted external JA. Existing production queue/worker orchestration remains, unattended operation not closed.
- New minimum core: evidence reconciliation on ASR-first candidate timeline, missing/rejected JA route, conservative gap/uncertainty decisions, source-bound JA master, source-derived identities and proof compatibility, supported speaker evidence integration. Optional targeted preprocessing only if retained evidence demonstrates worthwhile benefit; fallback to original audio. Do not reimplement working transport, worker, translator, publisher or develop unrelated models.
- Proposed execution order after explicit approval and release: (V2-A) one retained HSODA-104 source-chain diagnosis, no model runs, establish failure origin and compare source authority candidates; (V2-B) minimum isolated JA-master/evidence-routing candidate, focused offline invariants and representative regression; (V2-C) bounded private translation comparison using existing baseline, current A result as control plus ASR-first candidate, compare local-only variant where it resolves material decision; no whole-title ASR rerun without demonstrated need; (V2-D) integrate supported speaker/boundary and targeted improvements only against demonstrated failures, freeze optional research if inconclusive; (V2-E) one private end-to-end quality gate, then separately approved delivery/current repair and actual representative unattended queue/recovery/publish. Order is proposed, not a replacement of frozen P0–P4 until user explicitly approves.
- Change discipline: one objective/exit condition at a time. Completed technical checks reused unless concrete regression. Start with one representative failure and one control; add a focused check only for a material unresolved risk. If test work repeats without new decision-relevant evidence, stop expansion and report scope, evidence, blocker, trade-off and one next decision. Distinguish local defects, optional quality uncertainty and genuine core blockers. No new architecture/task/model because an individual test failed. Record proposal/approval and actual changes in canonical contract/active roadmap; no hidden priorities from old handoff. User decides conflict, deployment and design change. No numerical quality thresholds invented; compare omissions/false additions/meaning/boundaries against independently supported reference where available, not SRT count or old KO as truth. No mandatory per-title listening in operation. Technical PASS does not satisfy product goal; retain unresolved quality honestly.

Approval would require resolving source authority/ASR-only acceptance, JA-master plus whole-title interface, moving coverage work before downstream speaker/formatting, and conservative uncertainty/terminal behavior. Until then original roadmap and temporary hold are unchanged. No code/deployment/inference performed.

## 2026-11-22 — V2 user approval

User expressly approved ASR-FIRST HYBRID V2. Contract 2.0.0 and top roadmap supersede prior design/order. No runtime or product change. Model Switch hold retained. V2-A read-only first; subsequent work scoped separately.
